C,IAAI,GAAG,CAAC,IAAI,KAAK,QAAQ;QAAE,MAAM,CAAC,CAAC;AACpC,CAAC;AAED,MAAM,YAAY,GAAuB,GAAG,CAAC,QAAQ,EAAE,IAAI,CAAC;AAC5D,MAAM,CAAC,MAAM,YAAY,GAAW,GAAG,CAAC,IAAI,IAAI,iCAAiC,CAAC;AAClF,MAAM,CAAC,MAAM,QAAQ,GAAW,YAAY,IAAI,IAAI,CAAC;AACrD,MAAM,CAAC,MAAM,SAAS,GAAW,YAAY,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC,QAAQ,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC,GAAG,CAAC;AAC/D,MAAM,CAAC,MAAM,eAAe,GAAW,GAAG,CAAC,QAAQ,EAAE,SAAS,IAAI,KAAK,CAAC;AACxE,MAAM,CAAC,MAAM,OAAO,GAAW,GAAG,CAAC,OAAO,IAAI,OAAO,CAAC;AAEtD,oDAAoD;AACpD,MAAM,CAAC,MAAM,aAAa,GAAG,GAAG,QAAQ,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,mBAAmB,CAAC;AAC1E,MAAM,CAAC,MAAM,eAAe,GAAG,GAAG,QAAQ,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,2BAA2B,CAAC;AAEpF,MAAM,UAAU,eAAe,CAAC,IAAY;IAC3C,OAAO,aAAa,CAAC,IAAI,CAAC,CAAC;AAC5B,CAAC;AAED,MAAM,wBAAwB,GAAG,yBAAyB,CAAC;AAE3D,8CAA8C;AAC9C,MAAM,UAAU,iBAAiB,CAAC,MAAc;IAC/C,MAAM,OAAO,GAAG,OAAO,CAAC,GAAG,CAAC,mBAAmB,IAAI,wBAAwB,CAAC;IAC5E,OAAO,GAAG,OAAO,IAAI,MAAM,EAAE,CAAC;AAC/B,CAAC;AAED,gFAAgF;AAChF,oCAAoC;AACpC,gFAAgF;AAEhF,0DAA0D;AAC1D,MAAM,UAAU,WAAW;IAC1B,MAAM,MAAM,GAAG,OAAO,CAAC,GAAG,CAAC,aAAa,CAAC,CAAC;IAC1C,IAAI,MAAM,EAAE,CAAC;QACZ,OAAO,eAAe,CAAC,MAAM,CAAC,CAAC;IAChC,CAAC;IACD,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,OAAO,EAAE,EAAE,eAAe,EAAE,OAAO,CAAC,CAAC;AAClD,CAAC;AAED,iDAAiD;AACjD,MAAM,UAAU,kBAAkB;IACjC,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,QAAQ,CAAC,CAAC;AACtC,CAAC;AAED,8BAA8B;AAC9B,MAAM,UAAU,aAAa;IAC5B,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,aAAa,CAAC,CAAC;AAC3C,CAAC;AAED,4BAA4B;AAC5B,MAAM,UAAU,WAAW;IAC1B,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,WAAW,CAAC,CAAC;AACzC,CAAC;AAED,gCAAgC;AAChC,MAAM,UAAU,eAAe;IAC9B,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,eAAe,CAAC,CAAC;AAC7C,CAAC;AAED,kCAAkC;AAClC,MAAM,UAAU,WAAW;IAC1B,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,OAAO,CAAC,CAAC;AACrC,CAAC;AAED,sDAAsD;AACtD,MAAM,UAAU,SAAS;IACxB,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,KAAK,CAAC,CAAC;AACnC,CAAC;AAED,6CAA6C;AAC7C,MAAM,UAAU,aAAa;IAC5B,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,SAAS,CAAC,CAAC;AACvC,CAAC;AAED,qCAAqC;AACrC,MAAM,UAAU,cAAc;IAC7B,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,UAAU,CAAC,CAAC;AACxC,CAAC;AAED,iCAAiC;AACjC,MAAM,UAAU,eAAe;IAC9B,OAAO,IAAI,CAAC,WAAW,EAAE,EAAE,GAAG,QAAQ,YAAY,CAAC,CAAC;AACrD,CAAC","sourcesContent":["import { accessSync, constants, existsSync, readFileSync, realpathSync } from \"fs\";\nimport { createRequire } from \"module\";\nimport { homedir } from \"os\";\nimport { basename, dirname, join, resolve, sep, win32 } from \"path\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"url\";\nimport { spawnProcessSync } from \"./utils/child-process.ts\";\nimport { normalizePath } from \"./utils/paths.ts\";\nimport { stripBom } from \"./utils/text.ts\";\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Package Detection\n// =============================================================================\n\nconst __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);\nconst __dirname = dirname(__filename);\n\n/**\n * Detect if we're running as a Bun compiled binary.\n * Bun binaries have import.meta.url containing \"$bunfs\", \"~BUN\", or \"%7EBUN\" (Bun's virtual filesystem path)\n */\nexport const isBunBinary =\n\timport.meta.url.includes(\"$bunfs\") || import.meta.url.includes(\"~BUN\") || import.meta.url.includes(\"%7EBUN\");\n\n/** Detect if Bun is the runtime (compiled binary or bun run) */\nexport const isBunRuntime = !!process.versions.bun;\n\n/** Detect the esbuild-bundled Node.js distribution. */\ndeclare const PI_BUNDLED_NODE: boolean;\nexport const isBundledNode = typeof PI_BUNDLED_NODE !== \"undefined\" && PI_BUNDLED_NODE;\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Install Method Detection\n// =============================================================================\n\nexport type InstallMethod = \"bun-binary\" | \"npm\" | \"pnpm\" | \"yarn\" | \"bun\" | \"unknown\";\n\ninterface SelfUpdateCommandStep {\n\tcommand: string;\n\targs: string[];\n\tdisplay: string;\n}\n\nexport interface SelfUpdateCommand extends SelfUpdateCommandStep {\n\tsteps?: SelfUpdateCommandStep[];\n}\n\nexport type SelfUpdatePackageTarget = string | { packageName: string; installSpec?: string };\n\nfunction normalizeSelfUpdatePackageTarget(target: SelfUpdatePackageTarget): {\n\tpackageName: string;\n\tinstallSpec: string;\n} {\n\tif (typeof target === \"string\") {\n\t\treturn { packageName: target, installSpec: target };\n\t}\n\treturn { packageName: target.packageName, installSpec: target.installSpec ?? target.packageName };\n}\n\nfunction makeSelfUpdateCommand(\n\tinstallStep: SelfUpdateCommandStep,\n\tuninstallStep?: SelfUpdateCommandStep,\n): SelfUpdateCommand {\n\tif (!uninstallStep) return installStep;\n\treturn {\n\t\t...installStep,\n\t\tdisplay: `${uninstallStep.display} && ${installStep.display}`,\n\t\tsteps: [uninstallStep, installStep],\n\t};\n}\n\nfunction makeSelfUpdateCommandStep(command: string, args: string[]): SelfUpdateCommandStep {\n\treturn {\n\t\tcommand,\n\t\targs,\n\t\tdisplay: [command, ...args].map((arg) => (/\\s/.test(arg) ? `\"${arg}\"` : arg)).join(\" \"),\n\t};\n}\n\nexport function detectInstallMethod(): InstallMethod {\n\tif (isBunBinary) {\n\t\treturn \"bun-binary\";\n\t}\n\n\tconst resolvedPath = `${__dirname}\\0${process.execPath || \"\"}`.toLowerCase().replace(/\\\\/g, \"/\");\n\n\tif (resolvedPath.includes(\"/pnpm/\") || resolvedPath.includes(\"/.pnpm/\")) {\n\t\treturn \"pnpm\";\n\t}\n\tif (resolvedPath.includes(\"/yarn/\") || resolvedPath.includes(\"/.yarn/\")) {\n\t\treturn \"yarn\";\n\t}\n\tif (isBunRuntime || resolvedPath.includes(\"/install/global/node_modules/\")) {\n\t\treturn \"bun\";\n\t}\n\tif (resolvedPath.includes(\"/npm/\") || resolvedPath.includes(\"/node_modules/\")) {\n\t\treturn \"npm\";\n\t}\n\n\treturn \"unknown\";\n}\n\nfunction getInferredNpmInstall(): { root: string; prefix: string } | undefined {\n\tconst packageDir = getPackageDir();\n\tconst path = process.platform === \"win32\" || packageDir.includes(\"\\\\\") ? win32 : { basename, dirname };\n\tconst parent = path.dirname(packageDir);\n\tlet root: string | undefined;\n\tif (path.basename(parent).startsWith(\"@\") && path.basename(path.dirname(parent)) === \"node_modules\") {\n\t\troot = path.dirname(parent);\n\t} else if (path.basename(parent) === \"node_modules\") {\n\t\troot = parent;\n\t}\n\tif (!root) return undefined;\n\tconst rootParent = path.dirname(root);\n\tif (path.basename(rootParent) === \"lib\") return { root, prefix: path.dirname(rootParent) };\n\t// Windows global npm prefixes use `<prefix>\\\\node_modules`, which is\n\t// indistinguishable from local project installs by path shape alone. Do not\n\t// infer unsupported Windows custom prefixes without `npm root -g` evidence.\n\treturn undefined;\n}\n\nfunction getSelfUpdateCommandForMethod(\n\tmethod: InstallMethod,\n\tinstalledPackageName: string,\n\tupdatePackageTarget: SelfUpdatePackageTarget = installedPackageName,\n\tnpmCommand?: string[],\n): SelfUpdateCommand | undefined {\n\tconst target = normalizeSelfUpdatePackageTarget(updatePackageTarget);\n\tswitch (method) {\n\t\tcase \"bun-binary\":\n\t\t\treturn undefined;\n\t\tcase \"pnpm\": {\n\t\t\tconst match = readCommandOutput(\"pnpm\", [\"root\", \"-g\"])\n\t\t\t\t? undefined\n\t\t\t\t: /^(.*[\\\\/]global[\\\\/][^\\\\/]+)[\\\\/]\\.pnpm[\\\\/]/.exec(getPackageDir());\n\t\t\tconst binDirArgs = match\n\t\t\t\t? [`--config.global-bin-dir=${process.env.PNPM_HOME || dirname(dirname(match[1]))}`]\n\t\t\t\t: [];\n\t\t\treturn makeSelfUpdateCommand(\n\t\t\t\tmakeSelfUpdateCommandStep(\"pnpm\", [\n\t\t\t\t\t\"install\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\"-g\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\"--ignore-scripts\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\"--config.minimumReleaseAge=0\",\n\t\t\t\t\t...binDirArgs,\n\t\t\t\t\ttarget.installSpec,\n\t\t\t\t]),\n\t\t\t\ttarget.packageName === installedPackageName\n\t\t\t\t\t? undefined\n\t\t\t\t\t: makeSelfUpdateCommandStep(\"pnpm\", [\"remove\", \"-g\", ...binDirArgs, installedPackageName]),\n\t\t\t);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"yarn\":\n\t\t\treturn makeSelfUpdateCommand(\n\t\t\t\tmakeSelfUpdateCommandStep(\"yarn\", [\"global\", \"add\", \"--ignore-scripts\", target.installSpec]),\n\t\t\t\ttarget.packageName === installedPackageName\n\t\t\t\t\t? undefined\n\t\t\t\t\t: makeSelfUpdateCommandStep(\"yarn\", [\"global\", \"remove\", installedPackageName]),\n\t\t\t);\n\t\tcase \"bun\":\n\t\t\treturn makeSelfUpdateCommand(\n\t\t\t\tmakeSelfUpdateCommandStep(\"bun\", [\n\t\t\t\t\t\"install\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\"-g\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\"--ignore-scripts\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\"--minimum-release-age=0\",\n\t\t\t\t\ttarget.installSpec,\n\t\t\t\t]),\n\t\t\t\ttarget.packageName === installedPackageName\n\t\t\t\t\t? undefined\n\t\t\t\t\t: makeSelfUpdateCommandStep(\"bun\", [\"uninstall\", \"-g\", installedPackageName]),\n\t\t\t);\n\t\tcase \"npm\": {\n\t\t\tconst [command = \"npm\", ...npmArgs] = npmCommand ?? [];\n\t\t\tconst inferred = npmCommand?.length ? undefined : getInferredNpmInstall();\n\t\t\tconst prefixArgs = [...npmArgs, ...(inferred ? [\"--prefix\", inferred.prefix] : [])];\n\t\t\t// pi.dev advertises releases immediately, so a configured npm age gate would\n\t\t\t// block the update. npm has no per-package age gate, so this also lets new\n\t\t\t// transitive dependency releases through. Managed installs avoid this.\n\t\t\tconst installStep = makeSelfUpdateCommandStep(command, [\n\t\t\t\t...prefixArgs,\n\t\t\t\t\"install\",\n\t\t\t\t\"-g\",\n\t\t\t\t\"--ignore-scripts\",\n\t\t\t\t\"--min-release-age=0\",\n\t\t\t\ttarget.installSpec,\n\t\t\t]);\n\t\t\tconst uninstallStep =\n\t\t\t\ttarget.packageName === installedPackageName\n\t\t\t\t\t? undefined\n\t\t\t\t\t: makeSelfUpdateCommandStep(command, [...prefixArgs, \"uninstall\", \"-g\", installedPackageName]);\n\t\t\treturn makeSelfUpdateCommand(installStep, uninstallStep);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"unknown\":\n\t\t\treturn undefined;\n\t}\n}\n\nfunction readCommandOutput(\n\tcommand: string,\n\targs: string[],\n\toptions: { requireSuccess?: boolean } = {},\n): string | undefined {\n\tconst result = spawnProcessSync(command, args, {\n\t\tencoding: \"utf-8\",\n\t\tstdio: [\"ignore\", \"pipe\", \"pipe\"],\n\t});\n\tif (result.status === 0) return result.stdout.trim() || undefined;\n\tif (options.requireSuccess) {\n\t\tconst reason = result.error?.message || result.stderr.trim() || `exit code ${result.status ?? \"unknown\"}`;\n\t\tthrow new Error(`Failed to run ${[command, ...args].join(\" \")}: ${reason}`);\n\t}\n\treturn undefined;\n}\n\nfunction getGlobalPackageRoots(method: InstallMethod, _packageName: string, npmCommand?: string[]): string[] {\n\tswitch (method) {\n\t\tcase \"npm\": {\n\t\t\tconst configured = !!npmCommand?.length;\n\t\t\tconst [command = \"npm\", ...npmArgs] = npmCommand ?? [];\n\t\t\tif (configured && command === \"bun\") {\n\t\t\t\tconst bunBin = readCommandOutput(command, [...npmArgs, \"pm\", \"bin\", \"-g\"], {\n\t\t\t\t\trequireSuccess: true,\n\t\t\t\t});\n\t\t\t\tconst roots = [join(homedir(), \".bun\", \"install\", \"global\", \"node_modules\")];\n\t\t\t\tif (bunBin) {\n\t\t\t\t\troots.push(join(dirname(bunBin), \"install\", \"global\", \"node_modules\"));\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn roots;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tconst root = readCommandOutput(command, [...npmArgs, \"root\", \"-g\"], {\n\t\t\t\trequireSuccess: configured,\n\t\t\t});\n\t\t\tconst inferred = configured ? undefined : getInferredNpmInstall();\n\t\t\treturn [root, inferred?.root].filter((x): x is string => !!x);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"pnpm\": {\n\t\t\tconst root = readCommandOutput(\"pnpm\", [\"root\", \"-g\"]);\n\t\t\tif (root) return [root, dirname(root)];\n\t\t\tconst match = /^(.*[\\\\/]global[\\\\/][^\\\\/]+)[\\\\/]\\.pnpm[\\\\/]/.exec(getPackageDir());\n\t\t\treturn match ? [match[1]] : [];\n\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"yarn\": {\n\t\t\tconst dir = readCommandOutput(\"yarn\", [\"global\", \"dir\"]);\n\t\t\treturn dir ? [dir, join(dir, \"node_modules\")] : [];\n\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"bun\": {\n\t\t\tconst bunBin = readCommandOutput(\"bun\", [\"pm\", \"bin\", \"-g\"]);\n\t\t\tconst roots = [join(homedir(), \".bun\", \"install\", \"global\", \"node_modules\")];\n\t\t\tif (bunBin) {\n\t\t\t\troots.push(join(dirname(bunBin), \"install\", \"global\", \"node_modules\"));\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn roots;\n\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"bun-binary\":\n\t\tcase \"unknown\":\n\t\t\treturn [];\n\t}\n}\n\nfunction normalizeExistingPathForComparison(path: string, resolveSymlinks: boolean): string | undefined {\n\tconst resolvedPath = resolve(path);\n\tif (!existsSync(resolvedPath)) {\n\t\treturn undefined;\n\t}\n\tlet normalizedPath = resolvedPath;\n\tif (resolveSymlinks) {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tnormalizedPath = ==== thinking

I see the implementation of `PI_PACKAGE_DIR` in `dist/config.js` (which is compiled from `src/config.ts`).
It's used to override the package directory.

The changelog mentions "Pi's Node is now only a fallback" because of a Nix package putting its Node.js first on `PATH`.
This is likely related to how the `pi` CLI or the internal tools handle `node`.
I'll look for where `PATH` is handled or where `node` is executed.
I'll search for `process.env.PATH` or `PATH` in the `pi-monorepo` directory.

,CAAC;QACxB,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC;QACH,MAAM,IAAI,GAAG;YACZ,SAAS;YACT,KAAK,EAAE,UAAU,EAAE;YACnB,MAAM;YACN,mBAAmB,EAAE,OAAO,CAAC,SAAS,EAAE,mBAAmB;YAC3D,8BAA8B,EAAE,QAAQ,CAAC,qBAAqB;YAC9D,iGAAiG;YACjG,wEAAwE;YACxE,KAAK,EAAE,WAAW,CACjB,QAAQ,CAAC,KAAK,EACd,MAAM,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC,WAAW,CAAC,MAAM,EAAE,MAAM,EAAE,KAAK,EAAE,OAAO,CAAC,SAAS,EAAE,KAAK,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC,OAAO,CAAC,SAAS,EAAE,KAAK,CAChG;SACD,CAAC;QACF,sFAAsF;QACtF,MAAM,WAAW,GAAG,MAAM,CAAC;QAC3B,IAAI,CAAC,MAAM,YAAY,CAAC,QAAQ,EAAE,EAAE,GAAG,IAAI,EAAE,WAAW,EAAE,CAAC,CAAC,KAAK,YAAY;YAAE,OAAO;QACtF,IAAI,CAAC,gBAAgB;YAAE,MAAM,IAAI,KAAK,CAAC,iDAAiD,CAAC,CAAC;QAE1F,MAAM,KAAK,GAAG,MAAM,QAAQ,CAAC,KAAK,EAAE,CAAC;QACrC,4EAA4E;QAC5E,MAAM,wBAAwB,GAAG,IAAI,GAAG,CAAC,gBAAgB,CAAC,YAAY,CAAC,GAAG,CAAC,cAAc,CAAC,IAAI,WAAW,CAAC,CAAC;QAC3G,OAAO,CAAC,MAAM,CAAC,oBAAoB,CAAC,gBAAgB,CAAC,CAAC;QACtD,MAAM,EAAE,IAAI,EAAE,GAAG,EAAE,GAAG,MAAM,4BAA4B,CACvD,QAAQ,EACR,KAAK,EACL,wBAAwB,EACxB,OAAO,CAAC,MAAM,EACd,MAAM,CACN,CAAC;QACF,MAAM,YAAY,CAAC,QAAQ,EAAE,EAAE,GAAG,IAAI,EAAE,iBAAiB,EAAE,IAAI,EAAE,GAAG,EAAE,CAAC,CAAC;IACzE,CAAC;IAAC,OAAO,KAAK,EAAE,CAAC;QAChB,4FAA4F;QAC5F,IAAI,MAAM,EAAE,OAAO;YAAE,MAAM,IAAI,uBAAuB,EAAE,CAAC;QACzD,MAAM,KAAK,CAAC;IACb,CAAC;YAAS,CAAC;QACV,MAAM,QAAQ,CAAC,KAAK,EAAE,CAAC;IACxB,CAAC;AACF,CAAC","sourcesContent":["/**\n * OAuth sign-in for remote MCP servers.\n *\n * Connections never start a browser flow on their own. They send the stored access token and, after\n * a 401, try the stored refresh token. When that is not possible they fail with\n * `McpOAuthAuthorizationRequiredError`, and the user signs in through `/mcp`, which runs\n * the authorization code flow (PKCE, a Client ID Metadata Document or dynamic client registration)\n * against a loopback callback.\n *\n * Credentials live in `<agent-dir>/mcp-auth.json`, keyed by server name and URL.\n */\n\nimport { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { mkdirSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { join } from \"node:path\";\nimport { oauthErrorHtml, oauthSuccessHtml } from \"@earendil-works/pi-ai/utils/oauth-page\";\nimport type { AuthProvider, McpFetch } from \"@earendil-works/pi-mcp\";\nimport {\n\ttype AuthorizationServerMetadata,\n\tauthorizeMcp,\n\tMcpOAuthAuthorizationRequiredError,\n\tMcpOAuthProvider,\n\ttype McpOAuthState,\n\ttype McpOAuthStateStore,\n\ttype OAuthCallback,\n\ttype OAuthCallbackPage,\n\tOAuthCallbackServer,\n\ttype OAuthChallenge,\n\ttype OAuthClientInformationMixed,\n\ttype OAuthClientMetadataDocument,\n\tparseWwwAuthenticate,\n\tstepUpScope,\n} from \"@earendil-works/pi-mcp/oauth\";\nimport lockfile from \"proper-lockfile\";\nimport { APP_NAME, getAgentDir } from \"../../config.ts\";\nimport { type AuthStorageBackend, FileAuthStorageBackend } from \"../../core/auth-storage.ts\";\nimport { mcpNamespace } from \"../../core/mcp-servers.ts\";\n\nconst CALLBACK_HOST = \"127.0.0.1\";\nconst CALLBACK_PATH = \"/callback\";\n/** Where pi.dev serves pi's Client ID Metadata Documents: `client.json` and `<callback ID>/client.json`. */\nconst CLIENT_METADATA_BASE_URL = \"https://pi.dev/oauth\";\n/** Redirect URI for refreshes when none is stored. Refreshing never redirects the user. */\nconst FALLBACK_REDIRECT_URL = `http://${CALLBACK_HOST}${CALLBACK_PATH}`;\n/** Access tokens this close to expiry are refreshed before they are sent. */\nconst REFRESH_SKEW_MS = 30_000;\n/**\n * Bounds each request to the authorization server, so an unresponsive one cannot hold the refresh lock,\n * delay shutdown, or keep a sign-in waiting for long.\n */\nconst OAUTH_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;\n/** A refresh lock that its holder stopped renewing (the process was killed) is taken over after this. */\nconst REFRESH_LOCK_STALE_MS = 20_000;\n/** How long to wait for another process's refresh: longer than a stale lock lives. */\nconst REFRESH_LOCK_WAIT_MS = 25_000;\nconst REFRESH_LOCK_RETRY_MS = 100;\n\nexport interface McpOAuthSettings {\n\tclientId?: string;\n\t/** Already resolved. */\n\tclientSecret?: string;\n\tcallbackPort?: number;\n\t/** Loopback redirect URI; see `McpOAuthConfig.callbackUrl`. */\n\tcallbackUrl?: string;\n\t/** Scopes to request, separated by spaces. */\n\tscope?: string;\n\t/** `client_name` for dynamic client registration. Default: `APP_NAME`. */\n\tclientName?: string;\n\t/** See `McpOAuthConfig.clientRegistration`. */\n\tclientRegistration?: \"dcr\" | \"cimd\";\n\t/** See `McpOAuthConfig.authServerMetadataUrl`. */\n\tauthServerMetadataUrl?: URL;\n}\n\n/** Where the loopback callback server listens and the redirect URI it serves. */\ninterface CallbackSettings {\n\t/** Address to listen on. */\n\thost: string;\n\t/** Host name in the redirect URI. */\n\tredirectHost: string;\n\tport: number | undefined;\n\tpath: string;\n\t/** The exact redirect URI, when the port is fixed. */\n\tfixedRedirectUrl: string | undefined;\n}\n\nfunction callbackSettings(settings: McpOAuthSettings): CallbackSettings {\n\tconst url = new URL(settings.callbackUrl ?? `http://${CALLBACK_HOST}${CALLBACK_PATH}`);\n\tconst address = url.hostname.replace(/^\\[|\\]$/g, \"\");\n\tconst port = url.port ? Number(url.port) : settings.callbackPort;\n\tlet fixedRedirectUrl: string | undefined;\n\t// A configured URI with a port is sent exactly as written, since servers compare it as a string.\n\tif (url.port) fixedRedirectUrl = settings.callbackUrl;\n\telse if (port !== undefined) {\n\t\turl.port = String(port);\n\t\tfixedRedirectUrl = url.href;\n\t}\n\treturn {\n\t\t// `localhost` is served on 127.0.0.1; browsers fall back to it when ::1 refuses.\n\t\thost: address === \"localhost\" ? CALLBACK_HOST : address,\n\t\tredirectHost: address,\n\t\tport,\n\t\tpath: url.pathname,\n\t\tfixedRedirectUrl,\n\t};\n}\n\n/** Scopes of both lists, each once. */\nfunction mergeScopes(...scopes: (string | undefined)[]): string | undefined {\n\tconst merged = [...new Set(scopes.flatMap((scope) => scope?.split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean) ?? ,CAAC;QACxB,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC;QACH,MAAM,IAAI,GAAG;YACZ,SAAS;YACT,KAAK,EAAE,UAAU,EAAE;YACnB,MAAM;YACN,mBAAmB,EAAE,OAAO,CAAC,SAAS,EAAE,mBAAmB;YAC3D,8BAA8B,EAAE,QAAQ,CAAC,qBAAqB;YAC9D,iGAAiG;YACjG,wEAAwE;YACxE,KAAK,EAAE,WAAW,CACjB,QAAQ,CAAC,KAAK,EACd,MAAM,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC,WAAW,CAAC,MAAM,EAAE,MAAM,EAAE,KAAK,EAAE,OAAO,CAAC,SAAS,EAAE,KAAK,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC,CAAC,OAAO,CAAC,SAAS,EAAE,KAAK,CAChG;SACD,CAAC;QACF,sFAAsF;QACtF,MAAM,WAAW,GAAG,MAAM,CAAC;QAC3B,IAAI,CAAC,MAAM,YAAY,CAAC,QAAQ,EAAE,EAAE,GAAG,IAAI,EAAE,WAAW,EAAE,CAAC,CAAC,KAAK,YAAY;YAAE,OAAO;QACtF,IAAI,CAAC,gBAAgB;YAAE,MAAM,IAAI,KAAK,CAAC,iDAAiD,CAAC,CAAC;QAE1F,MAAM,KAAK,GAAG,MAAM,QAAQ,CAAC,KAAK,EAAE,CAAC;QACrC,4EAA4E;QAC5E,MAAM,wBAAwB,GAAG,IAAI,GAAG,CAAC,gBAAgB,CAAC,YAAY,CAAC,GAAG,CAAC,cAAc,CAAC,IAAI,WAAW,CAAC,CAAC;QAC3G,OAAO,CAAC,MAAM,CAAC,oBAAoB,CAAC,gBAAgB,CAAC,CAAC;QACtD,MAAM,EAAE,IAAI,EAAE,GAAG,EAAE,GAAG,MAAM,4BAA4B,CACvD,QAAQ,EACR,KAAK,EACL,wBAAwB,EACxB,OAAO,CAAC,MAAM,EACd,MAAM,CACN,CAAC;QACF,MAAM,YAAY,CAAC,QAAQ,EAAE,EAAE,GAAG,IAAI,EAAE,iBAAiB,EAAE,IAAI,EAAE,GAAG,EAAE,CAAC,CAAC;IACzE,CAAC;IAAC,OAAO,KAAK,EAAE,CAAC;QAChB,4FAA4F;QAC5F,IAAI,MAAM,EAAE,OAAO;YAAE,MAAM,IAAI,uBAAuB,EAAE,CAAC;QACzD,MAAM,KAAK,CAAC;IACb,CAAC;YAAS,CAAC;QACV,MAAM,QAAQ,CAAC,KAAK,EAAE,CAAC;IACxB,CAAC;AACF,CAAC","sourcesContent":["/**\n * OAuth sign-in for remote MCP servers.\n *\n * Connections never start a browser flow on their own. They send the stored access token and, after\n * a 401, try the stored refresh token. When that is not possible they fail with\n * `McpOAuthAuthorizationRequiredError`, and the user signs in through `/mcp`, which runs\n * the authorization code flow (PKCE, a Client ID Metadata Document or dynamic client registration)\n * against a loopback callback.\n *\n * Credentials live in `<agent-dir>/mcp-auth.json`, keyed by server name and URL.\n */\n\nimport { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { mkdirSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { join } from \"node:path\";\nimport { oauthErrorHtml, oauthSuccessHtml } from \"@earendil-works/pi-ai/utils/oauth-page\";\nimport type { AuthProvider, McpFetch } from \"@earendil-works/pi-mcp\";\nimport {\n\ttype AuthorizationServerMetadata,\n\tauthorizeMcp,\n\tMcpOAuthAuthorizationRequiredError,\n\tMcpOAuthProvider,\n\ttype McpOAuthState,\n\ttype McpOAuthStateStore,\n\ttype OAuthCallback,\n\ttype OAuthCallbackPage,\n\tOAuthCallbackServer,\n\ttype OAuthChallenge,\n\ttype OAuthClientInformationMixed,\n\ttype OAuthClientMetadataDocument,\n\tparseWwwAuthenticate,\n\tstepUpScope,\n} from \"@earendil-works/pi-mcp/oauth\";\nimport lockfile from \"proper-lockfile\";\nimport { APP_NAME, getAgentDir } from \"../../config.ts\";\nimport { type AuthStorageBackend, FileAuthStorageBackend } from \"../../core/auth-storage.ts\";\nimport { mcpNamespace } from \"../../core/mcp-servers.ts\";\n\nconst CALLBACK_HOST = \"127.0.0.1\";\nconst CALLBACK_PATH = \"/callback\";\n/** Where pi.dev serves pi's Client ID Metadata Documents: `client.json` and `<callback ID>/client.json`. */\nconst CLIENT_METADATA_BASE_URL = \"https://pi.dev/oauth\";\n/** Redirect URI for refreshes when none is stored. Refreshing never redirects the user. */\nconst FALLBACK_REDIRECT_URL = `http://${CALLBACK_HOST}${CALLBACK_PATH}`;\n/** Access tokens this close to expiry are refreshed before they are sent. */\nconst REFRESH_SKEW_MS = 30_000;\n/**\n * Bounds each request to the authorization server, so an unresponsive one cannot hold the refresh lock,\n * delay shutdown, or keep a sign-in waiting for long.\n */\nconst OAUTH_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;\n/** A refresh lock that its holder stopped renewing (the process was killed) is taken over after this. */\nconst REFRESH_LOCK_STALE_MS = 20_000;\n/** How long to wait for another process's refresh: longer than a stale lock lives. */\nconst REFRESH_LOCK_WAIT_MS = 25_000;\nconst REFRESH_LOCK_RETRY_MS = 100;\n\nexport interface McpOAuthSettings {\n\tclientId?: string;\n\t/** Already resolved. */\n\tclientSecret?: string;\n\tcallbackPort?: number;\n\t/** Loopback redirect URI; see `McpOAuthConfig.callbackUrl`. */\n\tcallbackUrl?: string;\n\t/** Scopes to request, separated by spaces. */\n\tscope?: string;\n\t/** `client_name` for dynamic client registration. Default: `APP_NAME`. */\n\tclientName?: string;\n\t/** See `McpOAuthConfig.clientRegistration`. */\n\tclientRegistration?: \"dcr\" | \"cimd\";\n\t/** See `McpOAuthConfig.authServerMetadataUrl`. */\n\tauthServerMetadataUrl?: URL;\n}\n\n/** Where the loopback callback server listens and the redirect URI it serves. */\ninterface CallbackSettings {\n\t/** Address to listen on. */\n\thost: string;\n\t/** Host name in the redirect URI. */\n\tredirectHost: string;\n\tport: number | undefined;\n\tpath: string;\n\t/** The exact redirect URI, when the port is fixed. */\n\tfixedRedirectUrl: string | undefined;\n}\n\nfunction callbackSettings(settings: McpOAuthSettings): CallbackSettings {\n\tconst url = new URL(settings.callbackUrl ?? `http://${CALLBACK_HOST}${CALLBACK_PATH}`);\n\tconst address = url.hostname.replace(/^\\[|\\]$/g, \"\");\n\tconst port = url.port ? Number(url.port) : settings.callbackPort;\n\tlet fixedRedirectUrl: string | undefined;\n\t// A configured URI with a port is sent exactly as written, since servers compare it as a string.\n\tif (url.port) fixedRedirectUrl = settings.callbackUrl;\n\telse if (port !== undefined) {\n\t\turl.port = String(port);\n\t\tfixedRedirectUrl = url.href;\n\t}\n\treturn {\n\t\t// `localhost` is served on 127.0.0.1; browsers fall back to it when ::1 refuses.\n\t\thost: address === \"localhost\" ? CALLBACK_HOST : address,\n\t\tredirectHost: address,\n\t\tport,\n\t\tpath: url.pathname,\n\t\tfixedRedirectUrl,\n\t};\n}\n\n/** Scopes of both lists, each once. */\nfunction mergeScopes(...scopes: (string | undefined)[]): string | undefined {\n\tconst merged = [...new Set(scopes.flatMap((scope) => scope?.split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean) ?? ==== text

The updates regarding **Environment & Nix** in version 1.3.0 primarily addressed how Pi interacts with the host system's environment and how it is packaged via Nix. Here are the deeper details and the corresponding code logic:

### 1. Nix Package & Node.js Path Fix
**The Issue:** 
In some Nix package configurations, the system's Node.js was being placed at the front of the `PATH`. This caused Pi's internal shell tools (which need a specific Node environment to run) to incorrectly pick up the system's Node version instead of the version bundled with Pi.

**The Fix:**
While the specific Nix expression is part of the repository's release pipeline, the logic was updated to ensure Pi's Node.js is treated as the primary runtime, with the system Node only used as a fallback.

### 2. `PI_PACKAGE_DIR` for Nix/Guix Store
Pi introduced (and improved) the `PI_PACKAGE_DIR` environment variable. This is crucial for Nix users because Nix stores often use paths that can be difficult to resolve or may contain characters that interfere with standard path lookups.

**The Code:**
In `dist/config.js`, Pi checks for this environment variable to override where it looks for its own assets (themes, prompt templates, etc.):

```javascript
// From dist/config.js
const envDir = process.env.PI_PACKAGE_DIR;

export function getPackageDir(): string {
  // Allow override via environment variable (useful for Nix/Guix store paths)
  if (envDir) {
    return normalizePath(envDir);
  }

  if (isBunBinary) {
    // Bun binary: process.execPath points to the compiled executable
    return dirname(process.execPath);
  }

  return findNodePackageDir(__dirname);
}
```

### 3. Shell Tool Session Environment
Pi injects specific `PI_*` variables into the environment of any shell command executed by the LLM. This allows tools to "know" who is running them and what the current session context is.

**The Environment Variables:**
| Variable | Description |
|----------|-------------|
| `PI_SESSION_ID` | Current session ID |
| `PI_SESSION_FILE` | Absolute path to the current session JSONL file |
| `PI_PROVIDER` | Currently selected model provider |
| `PI_MODEL` | Currently selected model ID |
| `PI_REASONING_LEVEL` | Current effective reasoning level (`off`, `minimal`, `low`, etc.) |

**Implementation Example:**
When you create a custom bash tool, these variables are injected automatically unless disabled:

```typescript
// Example of custom tool environment injection
const bashTool = createBashTool(cwd, {
  spawnHook: (ctx) => ({
    ...ctx,
    // You can add additional variables like 'CI' to the existing PI_* set
    env: { ...ctx.env, CI: "1" },
  }),
});
```

### 4. Clipboard & Remote Session Fix (`PI_CLIPBOARD_OSC52`)
For users running Pi over SSH or via multiplexers (like `tmux` or `screen`), standard clipboard operations often fail.
*   **Detail:** Pi now supports `PI_CLIPBOARD_OSC52=1`.
*   **Function:** When set, Pi sends an **OSC 52** escape sequence to the terminal. This allows the terminal emulator (like Kitty, WezTerm, or iTerm2) to handle the clipboard copy, even if Pi is running on a remote machine without a local X11/Wayland display.

# Pi v1.3.0 Release Summary

This document summarizes the key updates, features, and environment improvements introduced in Pi v1.3.0.

## 🚀 Key New Features
- **Flexible Session Sharing**: Users can now specify where `/share` uploads are stored using `sessionShareDestination` (options: `auto`, `gist`, or `radius`).
- **Prompt Cache Preservation**: 
    - Changing the thinking level mid-session on **OpenAI GPT-6** models now preserves the prompt cache.
    - **Kimi Code** tools added mid-conversation no longer invalidate the prompt cache.
- **Clean Model Switching**: When switching models, reasoning blocks from previous models are now dropped rather than being passed as text to the new model, preventing reasoning "leakage" into the visible answer.
- **Resumed Sessions**: Sessions can now persist active tools across resumes.

## 🛠 Nix & Environment Deep Dive
Significant improvements were made to support users in specialized environments, particularly those using Nix or remote connections:

### Nix Package Improvements
- **Node.js Path Fix**: Fixed a bug where the Nix package would place its version of Node.js at the front of the `PATH`. This previously caused Pi's internal shell tools to use the wrong Node runtime. Pi's Node.js is now correctly treated as a fallback.
- **Nix Flake Build Stability**: Fixed issues where Nix flake builds would fail when provider APIs switched. The model catalog pin is now refreshed automatically when model API groups differ, ensuring consistent builds.
- **`PI_PACKAGE_DIR`**: This environment variable is specifically optimized for **Nix/Guix** store paths. It allows users to override the package directory to ensure Pi correctly locates its bundled assets (themes, prompts, etc.) in complex store structures.

### Remote & Environment Variables
- **Remote Clipboard Support**: Introduced `PI_CLIPBOARD_OSC52=1`. This enables Pi to send OSC 52 sequences, allowing clipboard copies to work correctly over SSH or when using terminal multiplexers (like `tmux` or `screen`) attached from remote machines.
- **Session Environment Variables**: Commands run by Pi's shell tools now receive a suite of `PI_*` variables:
    - `PI_SESSION_ID`: Unique ID for the current session.
    - `PI_SESSION_FILE`: Path to the session JSONL file.
    - `PI_PROVIDER` & `PI_MODEL`: Current model selection.
    - `PI_REASONING_LEVEL`: Current reasoning level (e.g., `high`, `max`).
- **Process Markers**: Pi sets `AI_AGENT=pi` and `PI_CODING_AGENT=true` for child processes, allowing tools to identify that they are running inside the Pi agent harness.

## 🔧 Stability & Provider Fixes
- **MCP OAuth Hardening**: Improved security for MCP connections, including better handling of refresh tokens and scope escalation.
- **Codemode Robustness**: 
    - Improved error reporting in codemode scripts to help the LLM recover from failures.
    - Added support for images in `models.classify()` and `models.generateImages()`.
    - Fixed memory issues when running scripts that print in large loops.
- **Provider Updates**: Fixed numerous issues across Anthropic (Bedrock/Claude), Google (Vertex/Gemini), and OpenRouter, including incorrect context window reporting and thinking level handling.

# Pi v1.3.0 Release Summary

This document summarizes the key updates, features, and environment improvements introduced in Pi v1.3.0.

## 🚀 Key New Features
- **Flexible Session Sharing**: Users can now specify where `/share` uploads are stored using `sessionShareDestination` (options: `auto`, `gist`, or `radius`).
- **Prompt Cache Preservation**: 
    - Changing the thinking level mid-session on **OpenAI GPT-6** models now preserves the prompt cache.
    - **Kimi Code** tools added mid-conversation no longer invalidate the prompt cache.
- **Clean Model Switching**: When switching models, reasoning blocks from previous models are now dropped rather than being passed as text to the new model, preventing reasoning "leakage" into the visible answer.
- **Resumed Sessions**: Sessions can now persist active tools across resumes.

## 🛠 Nix & Environment Deep Dive
Significant improvements were made to support users in specialized environments, particularly those using Nix or remote connections:

### Nix Package Improvements
- **Node.js Path Fix**: Fixed a bug where the Nix package would place its version of Node.js at the front of the `PATH`. This previously caused Pi's internal shell tools to use the wrong Node runtime. Pi's Node.js is now correctly treated as a fallback.
- **Nix Flake Build Stability**: Fixed issues where Nix flake builds would fail when provider APIs switched. The model catalog pin is now refreshed automatically when model API groups differ, ensuring consistent builds.
- **`PI_PACKAGE_DIR`**: This environment variable is specifically optimized for **Nix/Guix** store paths. It allows users to override the package directory to ensure Pi correctly locates its bundled assets (themes, prompts, etc.) in complex store structures.

### Remote & Environment Variables
- **Remote Clipboard Support**: Introduced `PI_CLIPBOARD_OSC52=1`. This enables Pi to send OSC 52 sequences, allowing clipboard copies to work correctly over SSH or when using terminal multiplexers (like `tmux` or `screen`) attached from remote machines.
- **Session Environment Variables**: Commands run by Pi's shell tools now receive a suite of `PI_*` variables:
    - `PI_SESSION_ID`: Unique ID for the current session.
    - `PI_SESSION_FILE`: Path to the session JSONL file.
    - `PI_PROVIDER` & `PI_MODEL`: Current model selection.
    - `PI_REASONING_LEVEL`: Current reasoning level (e.g., `high`, `max`).
- **Process Markers**: Pi sets `AI_AGENT=pi` and `PI_CODING_AGENT=true` for child processes, allowing tools to identify that they are running inside the Pi agent harness.

## 🔧 Stability & Provider Fixes
- **MCP OAuth Hardening**: Improved security for MCP connections, including better handling of refresh tokens and scope escalation.
- **Codemode Robustness**: 
    - Improved error reporting in codemode scripts to help the LLM recover from failures.
    - Added support for images in `models.classify()` and `models.generateImages()`.
    - Fixed memory issues when running scripts that print in large loops.
- **Provider Updates**: Fixed numerous issues across Anthropic (Bedrock/Claude), Google (Vertex/Gemini), and OpenRouter, including incorrect context window reporting and thinking level handling.
